Detected mechanically from the cut's OWN authored `delivery`/`voiceTreatment` text naming it as a hum/sing-song (`cb_qa._is_motion_exempt_vocal`) — never inferred from the dialogue text itself, since that would be exactly the fragile, vibes-based signal rule 17 already rejects for vision checks, applied here to a text lint instead. Implemented in both places the law is stated: `cb_qa.check_camera_lock_conflict` (the QA lint, Law 8) and `cb_segprompt._v4_camera_discipline` (the actual shipped-prompt sentence, now "locked whenever a character speaks a line (a continuous hum or sing-song is not a lock trigger)"). Found live: 1.B1's own cut 2 ("Nailed it.", a real spoken line landing under a "push-in" framing) was a genuine Law 8 violation this amendment does NOT excuse — fixed separately, on the merits, by staging the wingman camera to lock exactly on the pose-straighten before the line lands (Julian's same review, amendment THREE) — the hum exemption only ever applies to a cut whose OWN dialogue is declared a hum/sing-song, checked per-cut, not per-beat.

39. THE PLATE IS A STANDING ANCHOR, NOT A RELAY-ONLY ONE (Julian's ruling, 2026-07-06 — "scene 1, beat 1 should have the scene slate, Fuzzby, Zenny, and the keyframe that's been signed off for beat one. There shouldn't be any re-mint in scene 1, in any first opening shot of a scene, because it's starting the role."): confirmed live that the scene's opening beat was never re-minted — that part was already correct by construction (rule 21: a beat's own Gate-2b keyframe is used only when it has no signed predecessor, no harvest/re-mint machinery ever touches it). But its actual FIRE was missing the scene plate as a reference entirely — `cb_beats.run`'s image list only appended the plate `if relay_status == "relay"`, and the same gate was baked into the v4 template itself (`emit_v4`'s `plate_n = (len(cast) + 2) if relay else None`, and `_v4_references`'s `if relay and plate_n:`), plus the two retired-but-reachable v3 fallbacks (`emit_prose_v3`, `emit_json_v3`) and `_v3_environment`'s own internal gate — found and fixed in the same sweep. The plate anchors the world's canonical look/palette/light for the WHOLE clip regardless of which beat is firing — a single keyframe only shows what's visible in that one frame, and the plate covers whatever the camera reveals beyond it; by the time any beat's own opening image exists, Gate 2a (the plate) is already signed too, since `cb_scene.keyframe_for` already composites the plate into the scene-opener's own keyframe for exactly this reason. Now unconditional in every one of those six sites: `emit_v4`, `_v4_references`, `emit_prose_v3`, `emit_json_v3`, `_v3_environment`, and `cb_beats.run`'s `imgs` list. Verified live: 1.B1's shipped prompt now carries `@图4: this scene's lighting, palette and environmental texture` — matching the reference stack Julian described exactly (plate, Fuzzby, Zenny, the signed keyframe, plus @Audio1 for the voice) — while 1.B2's relay behaviour is confirmed unchanged. Golden set now diffs further (expected, same already-flagged bucket as the manifest-authoring pass — not re-blessed without Julian seeing it first).

40. THE DEFINITIVE BUILD — CONSOLIDATE, PURGE, PROVE (Julian's ruling, 2026-07-06, "consolidate, purge, prove... this supersedes all prior partial instructions"). ONE — `PRODUCTION_DOCTRINE.md` rewritten as the SOLE stage-map document (Stage 0-7, exactly as Julian specified: script-in → Director pass/Gate 1 → world/Gate 2a → voices → keyframes/Gate 2b → the walk/Gate 3 (Scene-Opener Stack Law, cut-default relay, v4-sole-author, one-render economy, approval-not-file-existence resume) → Gate 4 retakes by timecode → Gate 5 post) — `REPLICATOR.md` retired into it, deleted (`git rm`), its two live-code mentions (`cb_replicator.py`'s module docstring, `cb_qa.py`'s twelve-law-lint comment) repointed. TWO — v1 (`for_beat`)/v2 (`for_beat_v2`)/v3 (`for_beat_v3`, both its prose and JSON emitters, plus every v1/v2/v3-only helper — `_action_for`/`_audio_for`/`_scene_v2`/`_action_v2`/`_speaker_map`/`_audio_v2`/`_v3_shots`/`_v3_settle`/`_v3_subjects`/`_v3_prev_frame_content`/`_v3_environment`/`_v3_tone`/`_v3_shot_sounds`/`_v3_speaker_map`/`_v3_gag_locks`/`_gag_lock_for`/`_v3_rule`/`_v3_expression`/`_v3_camera_end`/`_v3_constraints`/`_delabel`/`_short_role`/`_scene_for`, plus the dead `WING_LAW`/`_WING_LAW_ONE_LINE`/`_REFLAW_HEAD`/`_REFLAW_TAIL`/`CAMERA_GENERIC`/`NEGATIVES_GENERIC`/`_BEE_NEG`/`_RELAY_OPEN_LOCK`/`_RELAY_CAMERA_OPEN_LOCK`/`_CANONICAL_FRAME_DECLARATION`/`_V4_IDLE` constants they alone used) are DELETED from `cb_segprompt.py`, not merely deprecated — verified via an AST call-graph reachability check from `for_beat_v4`/`emit_v4` (catching every real dependency, including the two functions genuinely SHARED across the v3/v4 boundary despite their name — `_v3_style`/`_v3_ambience`/`_v3_negatives`, renamed `_style`/`_v3_ambience` (kept)/`_standing_negatives` for clarity) plus a manual check for module-level (non-function-body) call sites the AST scan can't see (`_load_chars`, called once at import time to populate `_CHARS`). `shipped_prompt` no longer falls back through a v3/v2/v1 chain — an empty v4 result surfaces as an empty prompt, not a degrade to a weaker builder. Cross-module orphaned references fixed in the same sweep: `cb_golden.py`'s direct `CS._v3_negatives(...)` call (now `CS._standing_negatives(...)`) and its dead, never-called `_name_free_chunks` helper (removed); `test_gate_cascade.py`'s scratch fixtures (found, mid-purge, to have been passing WITHOUT ever really exercising `cb_preflight`'s scene-cache-sync check — see rule 39's sibling fix — because the scratch episode had no `config/locations.json` entry at all; fixed with a `_sync_scratch_scene_cache` helper that injects a matching synced entry, so the test now correctly isolates cascade-relock mechanics without also tripping an unrelated, newly-functional gate). Verified byte-identical output for 1.B1's shipped prompt before/after the purge; confirmed no multi-seed remnants exist (only historical comments describing their prior removal) and no re-mint call site exists outside the one lawful, coded exception (`seamless_continuation` next beats) — the "degraded-harvest quality" second exception Julian named is NOT YET BUILT (no code path exercises it; flagged, not invented).
    THREE (prove it) and FOUR (the live sequence) are IN PROGRESS as of this rule, not complete: several pieces of Stage 5/6's doctrine as written are NEW-BUILD, not yet implemented — the `approval` per-beat data field, `media/rejected/` + `.REJECTED.json` sidecar archiving, `walk_scene` resuming on approval status rather than file existence, `cb_preflight.check_opener_stack`'s BLOCK, and Stage 6's whole timecode-retake subsystem (`assemble_review_cut`, `retake_at_timecode`). The doctrine document states the TARGET architecture (rule 7 — code and docs update together); building the target is separate, deliberate work from writing down what it should be, not yet done. Recorded here rather than silently claimed complete.

41. THE INFRASTRUCTURE FREEZE + THE ARCHIVE SAFEGUARD (Julian's ruling, 2026-07-06, choosing the live sequence over building Stage 5/6 first): Stage 5/6's new infrastructure is logged as the named post-Scene-1 build, `LAB_BACKLOG.md` item 0 — not built now. From this moment, a bug found triages on ONE question: does it block the next render? If yes, the minimal fix, nothing more. If no, it goes to the backlog — no more sweeping, no more adversarial audits, no more purge-adjacent cleanup, until Scene 1 actually has footage. THE SAFEGUARD standing in for approval-status resume in the meantime: before any beat in a scene fires under today's file-existence-based resume, every EXISTING take/harvest/settle-frame/derived-keyframe for that scene's beats is archived out of `media/` first (`media/archive/<episode>_<scene>_pre_<label>/`, never deleted) — so `os.path.exists(...)` finds a genuinely clean disk and cannot chain off pre-amendment footage. The scene's Gate-2a plate and the scene-opener's own signed Gate-2b keyframe are the only Scene-1 media NOT archived by this rule — they're the still-valid, still-signed anchors everything fires from, not stale takes.

42. THE V5 ENGINE — THE PERMANENT PROMPT COMPILER (Julian's ruling, 2026-07-06, after the third consecutive felt-intent rejection of 1.B1 — "we have to stop this is just awful" — followed immediately by "Build the v5 engine... superseding all prior emitter modes"): `cb_segprompt.emit_v5`/`for_beat_v5`, reached only through `shipped_prompt()`, supersedes v4 (rule 30) the same way v4 superseded v3 — a full purge, not a fallback chain (v4's own functions, `emit_v4`/`for_beat_v4`/`_v4_state_carry`/`_v4_references`/`_v4_timing_clock`/`_v4_settle_text`/`_v4_speaker_order`/`_v4_audio_prohibition`/`_v4_opens_on`/`_v4_camera_discipline`/`_v4_acting_rules`/`_v4_continuity`/`_v4_prohibited`/`_v4_opener`, are DELETED from `cb_segprompt.py`; the two genuinely-shared utilities survive renamed without the prefix, `_v4_species`→`_species`, `_v4_scene_noun`→`_scene_noun`, confirmed via grep that no other module called any `_v4_*` name directly).
    FIVE BLOCKS, ASSEMBLED FROM DATA, ZERO PER-BEAT AUTHORING (Julian's own spec, verbatim structure): (1) STYLE — the show's style law, verbatim. (2) REFERENCES — one terse line per @图N/@Audio1, the same opener/relay/junction-type stack logic v4 used (rules 21/31/39 — the plate stays a standing anchor, @Video1 stays motion-only, @图1's job still branches on junction type), reworded shorter for the word budget. (3) ACTING DNA — a NEW `characters.json` bible field, `actingDNA`, two lines of movement-and-comedy language per character, authored 2026-07-06 for all 11 named principal/recurring characters (the 2 bees plus 9 "bears" in the loose cast sense — Aida/Sunny/Luna/Misty/Amie/Howey/Keen/Keen's Mum/Squeaky; `Bo` excluded, a stub with no bible at all, per its own T6 "do NOT invent these" note) — pulled mechanically per cast member, raising `ManifestFieldMissing` for anyone without one authored. Fuzzby's and Zenny's DNA text uses Julian's own capsule phrases from this ruling directly ("enthusiasm outrunning coordination, mistimed landings, instant proud recovery, mishaps his own and played as triumph" / "deliberate measured grace, comedy in the pause, the slow blink, the deadpan non-reaction"), expanded to full prose; the 9 others were drawn from each character's existing bible mannerisms/comedyEngine/staging fields, never invented. (4) BEAT STORY — the beat's own `storyBeat` as the spine (3-5 sentences, unchanged director prose), the vocal order (who speaks, never the words — Law 6, unchanged), ending on `endState`'s living settle — with GENERIC SPEED ADJECTIVES MECHANICALLY STRIPPED (`_v5_strip_speed_adjectives`, a real regex transform, not just a lint) — applying rule 33's adjective-chaos ban ("a generic frenzy word with no physical beat behind it... is BANNED as unreadable") as an actual edit: a bare "fast"/"hyper"/"manic"/"wildly" spends a word of the budget saying nothing a video model can act on, when the beat's own named actions already do the real work. (5) TECH CLOSE — duration/mode + the camera-lock law (rule 38, unchanged: locked on any spoken line, hum/sing-song exempt) + the negative line: the standing six (`_standing_negatives`, its own strings shortened for economy but never dropping a constraint) plus exactly two new always-on items, "no 2D animation style" and "no flat, static-feeling rendering."
    THE WORD BUDGET, ENFORCED WHERE IT BELONGS: 400 words is a HARD BLOCK, enforced in `cb_preflight.py` (`check_beat_word_count`, compiling the beat's actual prompt exactly as `cb_beats.run` would and counting it — never a separate estimate) — never in the emitter itself, which always returns what the data says and never self-truncates. 250 is the TARGET, surfaced only as an advisory FLAG when exceeded. Every emit prints its own word count: this module's own `__main__`, `cb_beats.run`'s per-beat log line, `cb_beats.gate3_dryrun`'s returned dict (`word_count` key).
    A REAL DESIGN CUT, FLAGGED NOT HIDDEN: a first draft of block 5 also folded in the beat's own `cameraArc`, the scene's locked `ambientBed` (rule 25), and `stagingProhibited` — none of which Julian's literal 5-block spec names — and it blew the 400-word hard cap on ALL FIVE Scene-1 beats (431-480 words). Rebuilt to match the spec exactly (tech close is duration/camera-lock-law/negatives only), which brought every beat clean under the cap (309-342 words) with zero hand edits. This is a REAL BEHAVIOUR CHANGE from v4: a beat's own authored camera direction and the scene's constant ambient bed are no longer stated in the shipped prompt at all. Read as deliberate simplification given how detailed and exact Julian's spec was, and consistent with the whole arc of this session — heavy, over-specified camera/negatives JSON has not been producing the movement Julian wants, and a leaner prompt trusting the beat's own story content is a genuinely different bet worth testing — but surfaced explicitly in the zero-cost proof report, never silently assumed. `_v3_ambience` (the ambient-bed reader) is deleted outright, matching this codebase's own no-dead-code discipline; a two-minute re-add if Julian wants it folded back in.
    THE PROOF, AT ZERO COST: `cb_golden.py`'s `current_snapshot`/`assertions` rewritten the same day for v5's plain-text five-block shape (no more `json.loads` — v4's JSON envelope is gone); `cb_golden.py diff` shows zero assertion failures across all 5 beats and all relay/junction-type variants (the text diffs themselves are all-expected, since v5's shape is wholly different from the stored v4 golden set — recapture waits on Julian's approval, per his own explicit gate: "Golden recapture on my approval of those five"). All 5 Scene-1 beats (1.B1 opener, 1.B2-1.B5 relay/`intentional_next_shot`) compiled clean through `shipped_prompt()` with zero hand edits and zero `ManifestFieldMissing` failures — the bar Julian set for "the engine is validated."
